C2 FRACTAL / FULL-YEAR RESEARCH / 2025

NIFTY: carry-over versus daily exit

1 January–31 December 2025 · 249 sessions · 93,060 minute candles · December warm-up · all three pairs use identical dates.

Theoretical index results, before costs—not options profits. SL and 2R are measured on the index. Entries assume immediate candle-close information. Data and timing limitations remain material; the second archive's results are shown below rather than treating a public feed as certified truth.

Primary archive: Technovus

Daily exit closes at the actual session end (normally 15:30 IST in 2025). Carry retains positions overnight. Both use CISD confirmation-close entry, exact C2 low/high stop, 2R target, and the same source-derived invalidation rules.

Data-source sensitivity: Kaggle v18

Both archives cover every expected minute. However, 6,847 rows (7.36%) differ, with a maximum OHLC-field difference of 8.5 points. Those differences can affect exact CISD crossings. The same strategy is rerun unchanged on the second archive, including its own December warm-up. A shared upstream supplier cannot be ruled out.

Checks and limits

  • No missing minute, duplicate timestamp, invalid OHLC, or timestamp/epoch mismatch in the primary 2025 data. Both inputs share the same complete calendar.
  • Checked NSE holidays, February 1 Budget trading, and October 21 Muhurat hours. No false overnight gaps or normal-session buckets are imposed on the shortened session.
  • Compared all 249 daily ranges with Yahoo and three dates directly with NSE files, including April 7. The three official open/high/low checks match exactly. Daily settlement closes differ from final intraday closes and are not substituted.
  • The uploader identifies an unnamed broker API. Every minute is not independently exchange-verified. Original files, hashes, license, cleaning notes, and differences are retained in the data audit.
  • The source-rule fixtures and replay audits do not run TradingView's Pine runtime. The port's causal differences from the original indicator remain explicit.

Execution rules

  1. Full calendar year 2025. Both modes start flat on January 1; December 16-31, 2024 supplies indicator warm-up only. One position per timeframe pair; pairs are independent, not a combined portfolio.
  2. Same CantoLab-derived closed-candle C2 sweep, run-open CISD level and C3/C4 invalidations. Entry is the first causally available CISD confirming close; early CISD waits for C2 close. Late C3 boundary confirmation waits for the later available close. No backfilled CISD-line fill.
  3. Pine is an indicator, not an order strategy. The close-price fill and exact C2 stop/2R target are explicit trading assumptions. Original lookahead, retrospective drawings and deletion of past confirmations are deliberately not reproduced.
  4. Bullish SL equals exact C2 low; bearish SL equals exact C2 high, without buffer or tick rounding. TP is exactly 2R. Only candles after entry can hit the bracket. Same-minute SL/TP ambiguity uses stop first.
  5. Daily exit: close any remaining position at the actual session close, normally 15:30 IST, and 14:45 for Muhurat. Do not enter at that closing instant. Carry: retain positions overnight until SL, TP, or explicit invalidation; the final session close can initiate a position. An open position at the year boundary stays open, with no realized P&L.
  6. Stop gaps fill at the next available opening price and can lose more than 1R. Target gaps fill at the target. Explicit invalidation exits at the next eligible minute open, after resting gap orders. Setup expiry/replacement does not liquidate an entered position.
  7. 2025 normal continuous sessions end at 15:30, before the 2026 closing-auction change. Saturday February 1 Budget trading and October 21 13:45-14:45 Muhurat trading are included. Bars anchor at each actual session open.
  8. No fabricated, interpolated or forward-filled bars. Unexpected missing execution data would leave a position unresolved and block later entries; no such gap exists in either checked 2025 input.
  9. Totals sum each trade in initial-risk units (R), without compounding or a capital assumption. Max drawdown uses realized R, not intratrade mark-to-market. Monthly results are assigned by exit month.
  10. Gross results exclude spread, fees and slippage. A separate flat one-index-point cost sensitivity is illustrative, not a model of options execution costs. No options premiums, strike selection, expiry or Greeks are simulated.
  11. This is a zero-feed-delay theoretical index-price study. Delayed dashboard quotes cannot achieve these historical fills. The public archives are community data, not independently certified exchange minute feeds.
  12. The two archives disagree on 6,847 of 93,060 yearly OHLC rows (largest field difference 8.5 points). Source sensitivity reruns all six configurations without choosing a feed or parameter by its profitability. Matching daily ranges do not validate every minute.
